    GEO-38 — F-clinical-1.5T cost probe (the clinical-scale body coil)¶

    Item briefs and execution history (operator directive 2026-10-01). The §7 row is the status of record.

    Origin — operator directive 2026-10-14 (interactive session)¶

    F-human (0.15 m radius, 0.30 m long) is head-coil sized. Clinical body coils sit just outside the 0.70 m patient bore of wide-bore 1.5 T / 3 T scanners. The human operator directed:

    • F-clinical is the production fixture from here on for all new human-scale work (Phases 7 and 8, future ANS cases, clinically framed Phase 9 examples). The Phase 6 exit stays on F-human; F-human's records stay valid and are frozen as an intermediate fixture.
    • Two viable clinical coil models, one at 1.5 T and one at 3 T — 1.5 T first.

    The full directive, nominal geometry table and seven-step ladder are in PROJECT_PLAN §10, paragraph "Clinical-scale coil fixtures" (just before "Phase 7 — implants").

    Nominal geometry¶

    Fixture f Legs Rung diameter Shield diameter Rung length
    F-clinical-1.5T 64 MHz 32, high-pass ≈ 0.71 m ≈ 0.745 m ≈ 0.45 m
    F-clinical-3T 128 MHz 32, high-pass 0.704 m 0.743 m (shield length 0.70 m) 0.58 m

    Sources (found 2026-10-14 in the interactive session):

    • 3 T, 70 cm bore: a high-pass quadrature body coil 58 cm long, 70.4 cm in diameter, inside a shield 74.3 cm in diameter and 70 cm long — "Efficient high-frequency body coil for high-field MRI" (MRM), https://mri-q.com/uploads/3/4/5/7/34572113/body_coil_distriubtion_mrm.20177.pdf
    • A 70 cm-bore Siemens MAGNETOM Skyra 3 T modelled with a 32-rung high-pass birdcage — https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12137786/
    • 1.5 T: rungs 45 cm long on a body-coil carrier of 65 cm inner diameter; the RF shield within ≈ 2 cm of the end-ring diameter — US patent 11 442 125, https://image-ppubs.uspto.gov/dirsearch-public/print/downloadPdf/11442125

    The 1.5 T rung diameter (≈ 0.71 m) is set just outside the 0.70 m patient space rather than at the patent's 65 cm carrier (a narrower-bore design). The weekly may refine any value only with a cited source, and records which.

    Operator ruling 2026-10-15 (interactive session) — the port census¶

    32 legs, 64 sheets. A 32-leg high-pass birdcage has 32 ring gaps on each end ring; every one of the 64 is a lumped sheet (exact CAD rectangle, κ off). Census = 64; the untuned S-matrix at C3 is 64×64 (one factorisation, 63 held columns). Tuning and SAR (C4, C6) use the in-model route: the 64 gaps as series-RC sheets with the 0.2 Ω ESR, driven by two ports in quadrature. The "port census = 32" / "32×32" in the 10-14 text was F-human's 16-leg count carried over by mistake; 16 legs (keeping 32 ports) was considered and rejected because it contradicts the cited 32-rung source. This answers the dashboard's W7.

    Conventions (standing rulings)¶

    • Copper via Leontovich on the hole route (WF-12).
    • Exact CAD-rectangle capacitor / port sheets, κ off (2026-10-02).
    • 0.2 Ω capacitor ESR (2026-10-06, PORT-24).
    • Degree 2 default; degree 1 only ladder-licensed (2026-10-01).
    • Load: the ASTM F2182 gel box, 650 × 420 × 90 mm (MAT-21's fixture), placed as the standard places it. This fixture answers the dashboard's W5 (the ASTM box did not fit F-human's 0.30 m bore).

    Step 1 (this chunk) — the cost probe¶

    As on the §7 row: build the fixture, assert its CAD identities at -n 2, record cell counts at two mesh-size rungs, degree-1 / degree-2 unknown counts, and one measured degree-1 factorising column (wall time, memory), and state the degree-2 price as a prediction for the weekly to commission an xl / xxl window against. GEO-25 (the F-human cost probe, cost exponent 0.84 rather than r³) is the precedent and the scaling prior.

    Price the mesh before any solve: F-human at degree 2 is 2 570 024 unknowns and 89 GiB on xl (WF-12 H16, 2026-10-13); F-clinical is ≈ 2.4× the radius with a much larger load, so one degree-1 column may itself need xl.

    Later steps (for the weekly to enumerate)¶

    1. Mesh gates (port census, tags, exact sheets).
    2. The untuned 64×64 at degree 1 with PORT-11's reciprocity / passivity / symmetry gates at 64 MHz (operator ruling 2026-10-15, below).
    3. The in-model Larmor tune with ESR (the WF-10 route).
    4. Degree 2 on xl / xxl, priced by step 1.
    5. The ASTM box loaded: |B₁⁺|, 1 g / 10 g SAR, located hotspot.
    6. An ANS case for the operator's AED replication.

    Then the F-clinical-3T twin at 128 MHz through the same ladder.

    §9 briefs — 2026-10-15 03:00 daily review (chain C, steps C1 (a), C1 (b), C2)¶

    Drafted by a general-purpose agent for the 2026-10-15 03:00 daily review; the review reviews it. Licence: §10 chain C, enumerated by the 2026-10-14 weekly (PROJECT_PLAN.md:4855–4894); §9 rule (4). Binding scope for every item below: no tuning, compliance or scanner-specific claim (chain C, PROJECT_PLAN.md:4893–4894).

    Shared: the F-clinical-1.5T parameter set and what the generator can and cannot do¶

    What exists. MeshGenerator.birdcage_port_domain (src/fem_em_solver/io/mesh.py:4245–4283) takes every coil parameter chain C needs as a plain keyword: leg_count, ring_radius, leg_spacing (ring-plane separation, rings at z = ±leg_spacing/2, :5140), coil_length (the leg cylinders' span, :5225–5226), ring_gap_length (the high-pass layout), emit_port_sheets, ring_sheet_orientation="longitudinal", as_hole, resolution, conductor_resolution, air_padding, port_box_size. The F-human wrappers do not: FHumanBirdcageGeometry.generator_kwargs (src/fem_em_solver/study.py:440–466) and GEO-25's _params scale every length with s = R / 0.07 (study.py:404–411). At R = 0.355 m that makes the rungs 0.71 m long, the ring gaps 4.1 cm, and the floating leg boxes cross the shield. So the coil is a parameter set on the generator plus a new test module, not a wrapper call.

    What does not exist: a shield. grep -i shield src is empty. The outer boundary is the PEC air box (:5504–5511), which TH-14's _outer_box_tags pins as exterior ∖ cavity (tests/validation/test_th14_birdcage_copper.py:133). Item 468 adds a keyword-only shield_radius (sized below). The shield is then the cylindrical outer boundary, a closed PEC can: the lateral face at R_s plus the two end caps at ±z_extent. This is a modelling choice and the step claims nothing for it.

    Also not usable here. leg_sheet_strip_fraction needs leg_gap_length (:4560–4566), so it is not part of the high-pass layout. The ring sheets are the whole CAD rectangle chord × w (:4225, :4232) with κ off (ruling 2026-10-02).

    Leg count vs port census (for the review). The generator places 2·leg_count ring gaps (ring_port_count, :4206), so 32 legs give 64 ring-gap ports, not 32. The weekly's "port census = 32", "32×32" and "18 C16 × mirror classes" are F-human's (16-leg) figures. Every brief below uses 64 (and 34 C32 × mirror classes for C3: 17 same-ring + 17 other-ring separations). The review re-poses the census before queueing. The other option is 16 legs, which keeps 32 ports but contradicts the 32-rung source.

    The parameter set (module constant F_CLINICAL_15T_KWARGS in the new module; it is the single source of truth, as GEO-25's _params is for F-human):

    kwarg value rule
    leg_count 32 §10 table
    ring_radius 0.355 m rung diameter 0.71 m
    leg_spacing 0.45 m rung length = ring-centreline separation (the electrical rung between the end rings)
    coil_length 0.45 + 2 × 0.0321428571 = 0.5142857143 m F-human's leg overhang past the ring centreline, 0.015·s = 0.0321428571 m, held in absolute terms: the same leg/ring junction F-human meshes
    leg_width, ring_minor_radius 0.012, 0.004 m F-human's conductor stock, unscaled (study.py:425–426)
    ring_gap_length 0.0171428571 m F-human's absolute gap (0.008·s)
    port_box_size (0.0214285714, 0.004, 0.0214285714) m F-human's dx, dz. dy is cut from 0.01714 so that the floating leg boxes clear the shield. Their outer face is at R + 0.006 + 0.001 + dy = 0.366 m (:3946–3947). F-human's dy would put it at 0.3791 m, outside R_s.
    phantom_radius, phantom_height 0.10969 m, 0.65 m C1's load stand-in. The builder always meshes a phantom cylinder (:5253–5261). This one matches the ASTM box's volume (0.65 × 0.42 × 0.09 = 0.02457 m³ = π r² · 0.65) and its 0.65 m axial length, so C1's price includes the load and C2's air length. C2 swaps the box in.
    air_padding (0.03/0.07) × 0.355 = 0.1521428571 m F-human's margin, scaled with R (study.py:411, :456)
    shield_radius (new) 0.3725 m shield diameter 0.745 m
    as_hole, emit_port_sheets, ring_sheet_orientation True, True, "longitudinal" the copper hole route (WF-12)
    resolution 0.015 m F-human's, absolute
    conductor_resolution r1 1.6e-3 m / r2 2.4e-3 m r1 is F-human's sizing carried over unscaled. That is GEO-25 branch B: scaling the sizing with R is branch A, which fails CAD_MASS_GATE at 0.893 already at 0.15 m (GEO-25.md:3). r2 is WF-12 item 147's middle rung, which gave 0.7698 × the cells on F-human (WF-12.md:38).

    Derived closed forms. - Ring gap. α = g/(2R) = 0.0241449 rad < π/32. Chord 2R·tan α = 0.0171463 m. w = 2·0.004 + 2·0.001 = 0.010 m. Sheet area chord·w ≈ 1.71463e-4 m² (the module takes it from ring_port_layout, never re-typed). - Leg-arc clearance. 0.02028 m. - Air can. z_extent = max(coil_length/2, leg_spacing/2 + r_ring, phantom_height/2) + air_padding = 0.325 + 0.15214 = 0.47714 m (:5484). Can length 0.95429 m; volume π R_s² · 2z_e ≈ 0.41599 m³. - Shield clearances. R_s − max(R + r_leg, R + w/2, leg-box outer face) = 0.3725 − 0.366 = 6.5 mm.

    The 1.5 T shield length is not in the §10 table. The can length above follows from the air-padding rule; the weekly may refine it from a cited source.

    §9 item 468 — GEO-38 step 1a (chain C1 (a)): F-clinical-1.5T built, CAD identities at -n 2, cells and unknowns at two rungs¶

    Executor and cost. - Executor: implementer. - Tier: heavy by ceiling. Build-only, real (default) image, as WF-12 step 1's build windows ran (WF-12.md:50). W1 / W2 at -n 2; the rule-(c) re-gate at -n 1. - Cost: ≈ 10 measured (class median, chain no-solve) / ≈ 55 predicted min. That is 15 fixed + ≈ 25 code + ≈ 15 of windows. - Depends on nothing.

    Code. - src/ (ruling (c), disclosed). Add a keyword-only shield_radius: Optional[float] = None to birdcage_port_domain and _build_birdcage_port_model, ≈ 40–60 lines in io/mesh.py. None executes no new line. - Checks before gmsh exists: - Refuse with symmetry_quadrant. - Require shield_radius > max(R + max(r_leg, r_ring), R + w/2, port_radius + dy/2) + port_clearance. - Air domain: occ.addCylinder(0, 0, −z_extent, 0, 0, 2·z_extent, shield_radius) in place of the box (:5504–5511). - _face_on_wall (:5920–5937) also classifies the lateral shield face as a wall. Test: bbox x / y extents at ±R_s within 1e-6 and z-span 2·z_extent. The caps are already caught by the z test. - Diagnostics: shield_radius_m, shield_length_m, and air_domain_cad_mass_m3 (the cylinder's getMass before the fragment). - Test module. New tests/validation/test_geo38_f_clinical_build.py. - Selected only by FEM_EM_GEO38_STEP1A ∈ {r1, r2}, never by -k; skipped when unset. - Holds F_CLINICAL_15T_KWARGS and _f_clinical_kwargs(rung). - Lifts WF-12's _owned_census / _census_checks / _ring_sheet_areas pattern (tests/validation/test_wf12_f_human_hole.py:209–248) with ring ordinals 33..96 (range(leg_count + 1, 3·leg_count + 1)).

    Anchors (asserted at r1 and at r2; owned facets / vertices, allreduced — -n 2 is where a missing reduction shows). - (R) rung diameter. Every vertex of each of the 64 longitudinal sheets lies in the plane u = x cos φ_c + y sin φ_c = R. Assert max|u/R − 1| ≤ STRIP_BAND (1e-9, tests/validation/test_ops61_exact_strip_ports.py:58). The sheets are planar CAD rectangles; predicted ≈ 1e-15. - (L) rung length. Per sheet, (z_max + z_min)/2 = ±leg_spacing/2, so the separation is 0.45 m at 1e-9. Also, the leg-end planes satisfy max z over the cavity-wall vertices = coil_length/2 at 1e-9, which is the leg span 0.51429 m. - (S) shield diameter and length. - Every vertex on an outer facet with |z| < z_extent·(1 − 1e-9) has hypot(x, y) = R_s at 1e-9. gmsh places nodes on the OCC cylinder; predicted ≤ 1e-12. - max |z| over outer facets = z_extent at 1e-9. - The trap check, asserted. No cavity-wall (BIRDCAGE_CONDUCTOR_SURFACE_TAG) vertex has hypot ≥ R_s(1 − 1e-9), and _outer_box_tags' census reads outer + cavity = exterior with no tag collision (as test_wf12_f_human_hole.py:440–442). If the shield face lands in the cavity group, the copper wall term is applied to the shield and the PEC group shrinks to the caps. - (N) leg count. Take the cavity-wall facets with all three vertices at z = +coil_length/2 and bin them by round(atan2(y, x)/(2π/32)). Exactly 32 bins are non-empty, and every such vertex lies within r_leg·(1 + 1e-9) of its bin's axis (R cos θ_k, R sin θ_k). - (A) sheet areas. All 64 match ring_port_layout["ring_port_sheet_longitudinal_area_m2"] within SHEET_AREA_BAND 1e-9 (tests/mesh/test_birdcage_leg_offset.py:104). F-human measured 2.220e-15 (WF-12.md:438). - (C) ring-port census = 2·leg_count = 64. - 64 sheet tags, each with > 0 owned facets after reduction. - Cell halves 100+i / 200+i present for i ∈ 33..96. - The 32 floating leg boxes 101..132 are present. as_hole keeps them: emit_leg_ports=False is refused with it, :4555–4559. - No tag 1. - The census sums to the global cell count. - (U) unknown counts. U₁ = E_global and U₂ = 2·E_global + 2·F_global exactly, where U_k = functionspace(msh, ("N1curl", k)) global size and E / F are the global edge / face counts. This is the degree-2 Nédélec dof count on tets. F-human: 2 570 024 = 2·500 568 + 2·784 444 (WF-12.md:521).

    Negative control. - (c1) planted dropped facet (GEO-37 (c1), docs/planning/chunks/GEO-37.md:51–53), asserted by construction. On the lowest rank owning a P33 sheet facet, drop its first owned facet. Then |area_drop / (chord·w) − 1| > SHEET_AREA_BAND.     Review ruling 2026-10-15 10:30 (item 468 → item 478)¶

    Diagnosis (no log-pathologist: the reading is a CAD closed form, reproduced to the digit, and moves no status). The floating leg boxes are axis-aligned (io/mesh.py:5346–5356), placed at the leg-pair midpoints (2k + 1)·5.625° (:5337–5341) at port_radius r = R + max(r_leg, r_ring) + dy/2 + c = 0.355 + 0.006 + 0.002 + 0.001 = 0.364 m (:3946–3947). A box's outer corner is at ρ = hypot(|r cos φ| + dx/2, |r sin φ| + dy/2): at 5.625° with dx = 21.43 mm that is hypot(0.372961, 0.037678) = 0.374860 m — the W2 log's vertex (0.37296153, 0.03767824) and its cavity max 0.374859907 m (20261015T114056Z_GEO-38-s1a-W2-r2.log:13383, :13390; re-derived by this review). The same formula reproduces the leak census (boxes at 39.375° outside, 50.625° 0.09 mm inside ⇒ tags 101–104, 113–120, 129–132). The 03:00 brief's "6.5 mm clearance" was the face-centre value at 90°; no axis-aligned box meets it above the face-area floor dx·dz ≥ 2.5e-5 (:3938–3943; the best is 6.42 mm at dx = 1.167 mm). The new refusal on the branch used port_radius + dy/2 (branch io/mesh.py:4762–4775) and so passed the bad table; with a corner formula it would have refused before gmsh.

    Re-pose (rule (6) re-pose of the step-1 family: attempt 2 of 4; the step's target — rung Ø 0.71 m, rung length 0.45 m, shield Ø 0.745 m, 32 legs, the CAD identities, U₂ = 2E + 2F — is unchanged). 1. port_box_size = (0.004, 0.004, 0.0214285714) m — dx 21.43 → 4 mm (a square section, gap mode's dx = dy convention); dy, dz unchanged. dx feeds no anchor of this step: not port_radius, not the ring-gap layout (which takes no port_box_size, :4709–4719), not chord·w or the 64 sheet areas, not the PEC air extent (:5520, dy only). Face area 8.57e-5 ≥ 2.5e-5; the separation floor 1.25·max(dx, dy) = 5 mm vs 71.4 mm. 2. The design clearance is re-registered as ≥ 5 mm (a provisional modelling choice of the 03:00 review, not a band: it was never a weekly target). Corner max over all 32 boxes at dx = 4 mm: ρ² = r² + 2b² + 2r·b·(cos φ + sin φ) with b = 0.002 at 39.375° / 50.625° (cos + sin = 1.4074036) ⇒ ρ = 0.3668149 m, clearance 5.685 mm, binding on boxes 3, 4, 11, 12, 19, 20, 27, 28 (this review re-derived 0.366813 by components). The 5 mm target admits dx ≤ 5.77 mm. 3. The shield_radius refusal takes, for the leg boxes, max_k hypot(|r cos φ_k| + dx/2, |r sin φ_k| + dy/2) over the actual box angles, and refuses shield_radius together with leg_gap_length (chain C is high-pass only; simpler than a second gap-mode formula); for the ring-gap boxes, (R + w/2)/cos α (0.360105 m here, harmless, the same face-centre error); plus port_clearance. It exports leg_box_corner_radius_m. 4. New anchor (S′): the max vertex hypot over the cells tagged 101–132 equals leg_box_corner_radius_m to 1e-9 (a CAD identity; the boxes are planar-faced so the corner is a mesh vertex). The (S) trap check stays as written and must now read cavity max < R_s. 5. Option (ii), rotating the boxes, is declined: it edits F-human's default box construction (to stay bitwise it would have to be gated behind shield_radius), for ≈ 0.65 mm more clearance.

    Unchanged: every band, every prediction other than the cell counts (attempt 1's cells included the stray box cells and are not records), the 64-port census ruling (N_ports = 2·N_legs, the "= 32" returned to the weekly), items 469 / 470 behind this item (469 imports F_CLINICAL_15T_KWARGS and inherits dx).     Closed forms (drafter-computed; (K0) re-derives and prints them). - Leg boxes. - port_radius r = R + max(r_leg, r_ring) + dy/2 + c = 0.361 m (io/mesh.py:3945–3947). - Leg-box corner, the refusal's own formula (io/mesh.py:4815–4828): 0.363814913104 m, binding at the 39.375° / 50.625° boxes. - Clearance R_s − corner = 7.6851 mm, against the ≥ 5 mm design value (DESIGN_CORNER_CLEARANCE_M, test_geo38_f_clinical_build.py:70); 1.5 T has 5.6851 mm. - Ring-box corner (R + w/2)/cos α = 0.357105868 m. shield_need = 0.364814913 m < 0.3715, so the table passes the refusal. - Ring gaps. α = g/(2R) = 0.024350649 rad. Chord 2R·tan α = 0.017146246 m. Sheet area chord·w = 1.714624621e-4 m² (w = 0.010 m). - Can. - z_e = 0.325 + 0.025 evaluates to exactly 0.35 in float. - Volume π R_s²·0.70 = 0.303505 m³ (1.5 T: 0.415988). - Cap clearances: box ends 25.0 mm, leg ends 27.86 mm, ring tubes 56.0 mm. A context manager, active on rank 0 only. - It wraps gmsh.model.mesh.generate and gmsh.model.mesh.optimize, set with the test's monkeypatch so they are restored on exit. - A threading.Timer(stop_s) starts on entry to generate and is cancelled when optimize returns.     The page (docs/guide/clinical_coil_fixtures.md, Markdown, no tagged code block). Write "≈", never ~, and "the human operator", never a name. It quotes no AED figure; none applies. 1.     Review ruling 2026-10-30 18:00 (item 693's flag, entry 52's prerequisite) — C6a″ posed as item 694 under rule (6)¶

    The slot was right to hold entry 52. Item 693's "landing it ⇒ READY" was the daily's slip: entry 52's prerequisite (i) requires the readouts, and item 693 kept NotImplementedError. Item 693 stands DONE. The lattice stays provisional: the 10-28 weekly never ran (host outage, journal §1), so the 10-31 weekly ratifies or substitutes it.

    Reading the composition against the code found three defects in item 667's text. Each would have reached the xl window unexercised, because nothing in the module has run under 1:

    1. The sense. The 10-26 weekly's C6a and entry 52 say "the (1, +1j) quadrature drive" and "mean |B₁⁺|_ccw" in one clause. On an azimuth-increasing feed pair those are opposite senses: quadrature_phase_weights (test_birdcage_b1_quadrature.py:175–188) makes ccw the lagging pattern, (1, −1j), and WF-10 step 4 pairs ("cw", "(1,+1j)"), ("ccw", "(1,-1j)") (test_wf10_f_human_tuned_b1_sar.py:699, asserted at :243–244). I36b's _arm superposes (1, +1j) only (test_geo36_rod_f_clinical_drive.py:444). Ruling: the two feed columns are one factorisation, so both senses cost one linear combination each. The target stays the weekly's, "mean |B₁⁺|cw";he body forms bot* superpositions, runs the lattice under ccw, and prints (1, +1j) beside it at c* so the weekly's literal sense also has a number. The weekly rules which clause it meant; no gate depends on it.
    2. The coverage ball cannot clear the conductors. Item 667 said "with the conductor limit taken from F-clinical's ring radius in place of F_HUMAN_RING_RADIUS". The ball must contain the box: half-diagonal √(0.21² + 0.045² + 0.325²) = 0.389551 m, plus COVERAGE_BALL_PAD_M 1 mm = 0.390551 m, against 0.355 − CONDUCTOR_CLEARANCE_M 0.010 = 0.345 m. assert coverage_radius < conductor_limit (the WF-10 line, test_wf10_f_human_tuned_sar10g.py:514) would be a certain red, after the solve. That assert exists because WF-7's conductor was a bulk σ = 800 S/m volume. On a Leontovich hole mesh the copper has no cells, and off the gel σ = 0 and ρ = 0, so a ball past the rungs adds nothing to either side of the identity. Ruling: drop that line (item 667's own execution detail, not a gate); the coverage identity itself is the gate, unmoved, at EXACT_IDENTITY_RTOL. Assert instead that the radius reaches the farthest box corner plus the pad.
    3. Tag 403 is gel in arm R, but the density and the tagged mean do not know it. I36b's rho_field zeroes every tag but 3 (:535–537), which is right for arm M. In arm R, tag 403 carries gel σ, so a coverage ball counts its power while mean_sar(subdomain_ids=3) does not. The miss is about V_rod / V_box ≈ 1.5e-6 by volume, four orders over the 1e-10 band: another certain red after the solve. Ruling: in this module the gel is tags (3, 403) together: density_map zeroes only the other tags, mean_sar(subdomain_ids=(3, 403)), (i) on the union. The lattice is unaffected (every 10 g ball clears the rod capsule by ≥ 1 mm).

    No band, tolerance, done-when or target moved. The two dropped or re-scoped lines were item 667's. Provisional; the 10-31 weekly ratifies or reverts.

    §9 item 776 — 2026-11-03 (§10 C26, chain C / lane 1 — the circuit-vs-root sign, read; chain step (no solve))¶

    Executes §10 C26, with one premise corrected. The weekly's "C_mode with Z_RC" is identical to the r = 0 reading by construction. ring_c_modes (test_geo38_f_clinical_c_mode.py:138) reads C_k = 1/(ω Im λ_anti) on the bare Z. A uniform r adds r·I (ring_mode_rows(esr_ohm=), src/fem_em_solver/ports/ring_modes.py:187–212), which moves Re λ and leaves Im λ alone; ESR ruling (i) says the same. So C_mode cannot carry the sign.

    What can carry it is the circuit-layer root: Im Y_in at the feed under the terminations, computed from the saved 64×64 alone. It is the same quantity the in-model roots are read on, and C4's module already prints it as "circuit-only Y_in" (test_geo38_f_clinical_esr_root.py; line :12587 of entry 50's log). Locate that helper first. If it needs the field solve's per-node data rather than the npz, stop: the item is BLOCKED with the reason. The target is kept: a record that decides whether the sign difference is the ESR's or the fixture's.

    Measurement. On both saved matrices, which are present: logs/geo38/c3-cu-s64-d1.npz (64 MHz) and logs/geo38/c11-cu-s64-d1-128mhz.npz (128 MHz). - (a) C_mode(k = 1) by ring_c_modes.     Review ruling 2026-11-05 03:00 — chain C's slot steps (P61b, C6b, C34, C31, C32) as execution detail¶

    Writing the briefs for the 11-04 weekly's chain C slot steps, this review found seven premises in the step text that the code contradicts. Each is corrected as execution detail, with the target kept, and none makes a step unqueueable:

    1. C34: I36b's degree knob already exists. It is not FEM_EM_GEO36_S8_DEGREE but FEM_EM_GEO36_S8_DRIVE_DEGREE, wired through the whole drive branch (test_geo36_rod_f_clinical_drive.py:35–37, :62, :112–130, :219–225, :499–511, :553). It is refused under build, because I36b's build window already asserted (Deg-2) on this mesh: 8 327 394 = 2·1 606 744 + 2·2 556 953 (20261027T170559Z_GEO-36-I36b-W1-build.log:11989–11990). So I36b's half of C34 adds no knob; it is a stub proof of a degree-2 path that has never run.
    2. Entry 62 cannot be one pytest call. C6a's foreign guard refuses FEM_EM_GEO36_S8_DRIVE, FEM_EM_GEO36_S8_DRIVE_DEGREE and FEM_EM_GEO38_C4_DEGREE (test_geo38_f_clinical_box_loaded.py:55–65). Its command is two sequential pytest calls in one window, each with its own environment prefix. That is for the 11-07 weekly, which writes the command. C34 pins it with a test (K-62), and no guard is changed.
    3. C6b: a 50 mm grid anchored at the box centre cannot contain the lattice's x = ±Lx/4 = ±105 mm. The x step is therefore Lx/8 = 52.5 mm, and the z and y steps are 50 mm. "3D" gives one layer in y: the box is 90 mm deep, and a 10 g ball needs |dy| ≤ 30.6 mm. The grid is 91 candidates, of which 90 are kept; the one on the rod axis is excluded.
    4. C6b is off by default (FEM_EM_GEO38_C6_GRID). _run_box is also called by C21 (entry 58) and P61 (entry 59). Without a knob, both windows would buy the grid unpriced, against the weekly's "no degree-1 re-run is bought for it". The K8 exact key-set asserts (box_loaded.py:1031, 3t_box_loaded.py:536) also require it.
    5. C31 is serial on P61b (item 818). "Both senses as P61b's" is P61b's code.
    6. Entry 62's price leaves out the grid. Entry 52's readouts took 169.7 s for ≈ 20 full-mesh ball assemblies (20261103T080009Z_GEO-38-C6.log:12312), at most ≈ 8.5 s per ball at degree 1, -n 8. The grid adds 72 balls, ≲ 610 s at degree 1 (arithmetic, not a measurement). The degree-2 price is the 11-07 weekly's.
    7. |B₁⁺| is curl E interpolated at DG0 midpoints at either degree (src/fem_em_solver/post/faraday.py:58–62). So part of entry 62's degree-1 → degree-2 |B₁⁺| move belongs to the reconstruction, not to the solve. It is printed, never gated.
